- `inventoryBase({ census, folder, routing?, notDefects?, generated? })` → the unsampled inventory, built once; `withSamples(base, samples?)` → `{ animations, surfaces, occurrences, propVariants, samples, problems, gaps, meta }`; `buildInventory({ census, folder, routing?, notDefects?, samples?, generated? })` is the two in one call; `validateInventory(inv)` → master-schema problems; `reconcile(inv, { census?, jsWriters? })` → an occurrence with no record or a count naming a missing record, and with the census a counted row with no occurrence, an occurrence count that differs from the counted rows plus writer sites, and a trigger kind whose `CONTEXT_ONLY_MIN` (3) or more occurrences all fall back to `context`.
- `readTheirs(folder)` → Claude Design's `{ animations, surfaces }`; `readInventory(row, { rows, sources })` → the registry reading `{ recordIds, role, why? }` or null (the inventory is built once per pair of rows and sources lists); `inventoryStage(job, ctx)` → the intake stage's parts.

### `lib/intake-inventory-anim.mjs`
- `CATEGORIES` (the master schema's counted categories as census-row tests); a code-built style string's K record names the template elements holding its hole as `element.nearest_tpl_id`; `animationRecords(census, { notDefects? })` → `{ records, census: Map(row id → record IDs), sites, counts, unresolved, meta }`; `keyframeProperties(stops)` → one `{ property, from, to, keyframes? }` per property an `@keyframes` rule animates (a K record carries them after its `animation-name` row).

### `lib/intake-inventory-tpl.mjs`
- `elementStyles(element)`, `describeElement(comp, element)`, `traceOf(rows, { pseudo? })`; `templateRecords(census, add)` → TR, PS, K and RM records through `add`; `animationRecord(add, row, element, source, opts)`; `applyReducedMotion(records, rmRecords, metaOf)`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-code.mjs`
- `codeModel(census)`, `textOf(prog, node)`, `offsetOf(prog, draftId)`, `lineOf(node)`, `nodeAt(prog, off, re)`, `ancestors(prog, node)`, `declaratorsOf(prog)` (Map name → initialised declarators, built once per program or logic model), `memberOf(prog, node)`, `functionName(prog, node)`, `eventTrigger(event)`; the node tests `isFn`, `isThis`, `prop`, `globalName`.
- `triggerOf(model, comp, node)` → `{ type, detail, state_keys?, holes?, delay, via, members, called }` (`holes`: the renderVals key names a value built in code is passed to the template as); `reducedMotionOf(prog, node)` → `{ behaviour, detail }`; `literalOf(node)`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-target.mjs`
- `targetOf(model, comp, expr)` → `{ tpl, comp, selector, rule? }`; `constValue(prog, node)`; `waapiEffect(prog, call)` → `{ properties, timing, frames }`; `writerSites(prog)` → `[{ node, mechanism, target, properties }]`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-keys.mjs`
- `stateWriters(prog)` → Map(member → state keys written); `routeKeys(prog)`, `viewportKeys(prog)` → the keys a router or the viewport writes; `timeReads(prog)`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-css.mjs`
- `cssDecls` (re-exported), `ms(time)`, `splitTop(text, sep?)`, `parseTransition(value)`, `parseAnimation(value)` (the iteration count, `infinite` included, of a template style, a helmet rule or the quoted text of a style-object string), `cssBlocks(text)` → `[{ media, selector, decls }]`, `sameSelector(a, b)`, `reducedMotionMedia(media)`, `matchSelector(elements, selector)` → `{ tpl, rule? }`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-surfaces.mjs`
- `surfaceRecords(census, { model, anim, routing?, props? })` → `{ records, counts, unresolved, scIf, componentSurface, keySurfaces }`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-sample.mjs`
- `SAMPLE_WIDTHS`; `sampleTargets(records, meta)` → the interval and frame-loop J records with a template target; `sampleWriters({ folder, index, harPath, file?, hash?, targets, widths?, harness? })` → `{ samples: [{ recordId, width, t, values }], gaps }`.

### `lib/intake-inventory-diff.mjs`
- `norm(value)`, `pairKey(record)`, `routeKey(route)`, `scIfKey(key)`; `diffAnimations(ours, theirs)`, `diffSurfaces(ours, theirs)`, `diffInventory(ours, theirs)` → `[{ rowId, draftId, text, field, ours, theirs, why }]`; a row ID, and a record row's `ours` or `theirs`, is built from the pairing key, never a record ID, so inserting a record changes no other row.

### `lib/intake-props.mjs`
- `declaredProps(text)` → `{ props }` or `{ error }` from the `data-props` of `<script data-dc-script>`; `propVariants({ folder, census })` → `{ props: [{ component, prop, editor, options, default, mounted, variants, unused, unusedOptions, gates }], gaps }`.

### `lib/intake-review-carry.mjs`
- `withUnappliedMarks(previous, markdown, { day })` → `{ intake, carried, acceptAll }`: the previous intake with each unmarked row given its review.md mark, the rows carried, and the sections whose Accept all is ticked.

### `lib/intake-review.mjs`
- `ROW_TYPES`, `SECTION_TITLES`; `MARKS` (Appendix C's mark values per row type; the first is what "Accept all" sets) and `markShapes`; `DEFAULTED` (the row types whose blank Mark cell takes the first value: flag, not-a-defect); `safeRowId(id)` (`|`, a backtick, CR and LF written as `%7C`, `%60`, `%0D`, `%0A`, so every row ID survives the table); `UNWIRED_NOTE` (the reason cell of every not-a-defect row); `markProblem(rowType, mark)` → why a mark is refused, or null.
- `HASH_FIELDS`, `VOLATILE`, `sha`, `contentHash(rowType, row, draftId)`: the content hash of exactly what a row shows.
- `reviewRows(intake)` → `[{ rowId, rowType, draftId, contentHash, shows, proposed, note, acceptAll, needs, cause?, causeReason? }]` (`needs` is why a row needs reading, ranked by `NEEDS_ORDER`; a seen-but-unsupported missing-feature row, `from: 'coverage'`, takes `acceptAll` `gap:<wave>`, or `difference` when its capability is excluded, and no mark when the coverage map does not hold it; `cause` is the standing rule or client-built post that settled a skeleton row); `byCause(skeletonRows)` → `[[heading, rows]]`: the rows needing a decision by need and proposed block, then one group per rule or built post headed by its reason, then the confident proposals (`CONFIDENT_HEADING`), never one group per page; `readingCounts(intake, changed?)` → rows needing reading against rows accept-all covers, per section; page-map rows are `page:<postType>:<slug>`, notices `notice:prose:<file>:<line>` and `notice:host:<host>`.
- `renderReview(intake, { changed? })` → the `review.md` text (changed rows from `intake.review` when no list is passed); `parseReview(markdown)` → `{ sections, rowMarks, rowSection, problems }`.
- `applyReview(intake, markdown, { today })` → `{ intake, problems, missing }`; `isoDay(date)`. `carryOver(previousIntake, intake)` → `{ review, changed, realigned, refused }`: a skeleton mark whose row id now names another element, or is gone, follows its element by fingerprint (same proposed block) or is listed in `refused`.
- `unwiredRows(intake)` → the `notDefects` entries of the unwired dead-code kind as `not-a-defect` rows (`not-defect:<censusId>`, default mark `not-a-defect`); `notDefectLine(intake)` → the header line counting every other not-a-defect entry per reason (FR-48-34).

### `lib/intake-ast-eval.mjs`
- `ref(node, env)` → `{ node, env }`, a node paired with its loop environment; `returnsOf(idx, fn)` → a function's returned nodes, `propRefs(idx, node, key)` → where a property's value comes from.
- `resolveRefs(idx, node)` and `elementsOf(idx, node)` → the data a value or a loop iterates over; `staticTruth(idx, node, env)` → true, false or unknown for a simple filter condition.
- `evalDeep(idx, node, env?)` → `evalStr`'s result with loop variables followed; `loopEnvs(idx, node)` → one environment per loop item; `oneString(result)` → the single string or null.
- `trackWrites(idx)` → the same index with `writes` (values `o.k = v`, `o[k] = v`, push, unshift, splice and `Object.assign` add to an object or array literal the resolver reaches, which `propRefs`, `elementsOf` and computed reads then include) and `unknownWrites` (writes whose target it cannot reach); an index never passed through it reads only what each literal declares.

### `lib/intake-pagemap.mjs`
- `PAGE_MAP_SCHEMA`; `classifyRoute(text, { prices })` → `{ postType, template, collection }` for a route's content.
- `proposePageMap({ folder, routing?, census?, surfacesHint? })` → `{ rows, linkMap: [{ from, href, kind: row|external|control|gap, slug?, error? }], gaps }` (`surfacesHint` defaults to the export's `surfaces.json`; `null` skips the cross-check).
- `pageMapProblems(rows, ajv?)` → schema problems, duplicate slugs and parents naming no row.
- `drawerRepeatsHeader(menu, megaLabels)` → true for a drawer-region draft menu whose top-level items are each a header mega item's label.

### `lib/intake-pagemap-surfaces.mjs`
- `codeSurfaces(exp, routeOf)` → the CPT surfaces found in the code with their menus; `crossCheck(code, hint)` → rows on one side only, as gap rows. `KIND_POST_TYPE`: surface kind to post type.
- `elementTree`, `innerText`, `descendants`, `ancestors`, `kebab`, `stem`: template tree and naming helpers.

### `lib/intake-spec-sync.mjs`
- `SPEC_FILE`, `DATA_DIR`: the spec and the route's `data/` folder. `LABELS`: each Appendix C label with its schema file and shape (`body`, or `items` for the page-map array).
- `specBlock(specText, label)` → the parsed JSON block after `**<label>:**`; throws naming the label when there is none.
- `constructKinds(specText)` → the construct kinds Appendix C lists for the registry.
- `syncProblems(specText, label, schema)` → each disagreement between the spec block and the schema file (wrapper keys ignored), prefixed with the label.
- `intakeProblems(specText, schema)` → each intake-record key missing from the schema's `required` or `properties`, and each extra required key.

### `lib/motion-schema.mjs`
- `REPO`, `MOTION_SCHEMA_FILE`: the repo root and the schema path. `loadMotionSchema(file?)` → the parsed schema. `annotationKeywords(schema)` → every `x-sgs-*` keyword the schema uses (registered with ajv).
- `RANGE_UNITS`, `RELATIVE_UNITS`: the base-unit conversions (ms/s, px, deg/turn/rad) and the units a ranged field refuses. `rangeProblem(range, value)` → the error text for a value outside an `x-sgs-range`, or null (tokens and `calc()` are not range-checked).
- `motionValidator(schema?)` → the compiled ajv validator (cached per schema). `formatError(e)` → one ajv error as `path: message`. `schemaErrors(doc, schema?)` → every schema error. `checkMotion(doc, schema?)` → `{ valid, errors, refusals }`.
- `enumerateFields(schema?)` → every field with its `x-sgs-field` annotation (panel, field type, CSS property, state, per-device), the list the DB motion-field rows are seeded from. Re-exports `motionRefusals`, `elementRefusals` and `elementLayers`.

### `lib/motion-validate.mjs`
- `SELECTOR_MAX_LENGTH`, `SELECTOR_ALLOWED`, `SELECTOR_DENIED`; `selectorProblem(selector)` → the reason a custom-target selector is not scoped to the block root, or null.
- `customTargetErrors(doc)` → the custom-target rule failures; `validateMotionWrite(doc, schema)` → `{ valid, errors, refusals }` (the schema's errors and refusals plus the custom-target errors); `assertMotionWritable(doc, schema)` throws one Error listing every problem and returns the document when it is writable.

### `lib/motion-refusals.mjs`
- `elementLayers(element)` → `[{ list, path, effect, layer }]` in paint order. `layerLabel(schema, entry)` → the layer's display label. `slotClaims(schema, entry)` → the paint slots a layer claims. `matchedValues(entry, m)` → the rule side's value paths this layer sets (in values or a device override; with `nonZero`, a zero amount is unset). `sideLabel(schema, entry, m)` → the label a refusal names the layer by for that side (a `valueLabels` label when every matched value maps to one). `matches(schema, entry, m)` → whether a layer meets one side of a refusal rule.
- `elementRefusals(schema, element)` → the refusal texts for one element. `motionRefusals(schema, doc)` → `{ <target>: [texts] }` for every element of a document.

### `handoff-repackage.mjs`
- `parseArgs(argv)` → `{ folder, project, entries, generated, dryRun }` or `{ error }`. `main(argv, log?)` → the exit code.
- `readOldManifest(text)` → `{ rows: [{ path, bytes, sha256 }], runtime }` from an old `MANIFEST.md`.
- `proveUnchanged(root)` → `{ problems, notes }`: a problem is a file changed since export or a folder that cannot be proven unchanged.
- `repackage(root, { project, entries, generated, dryRun?, schemas? })` → `{ problems, notes, actions }`; writes nothing when `problems` is non-empty or `dryRun` is set.

### `lint.mjs`
- `routeFiles(root)` → every route file, relative.
- `exportsOf(src)` → exported names.
- `lintFolder(root)` → problems (README index, scripts imports, a file naming the plugin's `node_modules`).
- `lintTree(tree, db, label?)` → problems (core style, native_wp).
- `lintSkeleton(tree, db, label?)` → problems (style values; and a brand `sgs/icon` that types its own brand's address in `linkUrl` with no `metadata.bindings.linkUrl`).
- `registerIds(markdown)` → the item ids a fix register holds (table first cells, comma-split, and ids leading a bullet).
- `HOUSE_RULES`: ledger rules that are house rules (`touch-target`, `accessibility`): their entries cite no register item.
- `lintLedger(entries, ids, label?)` → problems: an entry (not a house rule) whose `register` ids are missing or not in `ids`.
- `lintSurfaceLedger(surfacesFile, registerFile?, repo?)` → `lintLedger` on `<build>/qa/divergences.json` against `registerFile`, else the register `qa/ledger.config.json` names (from the repo root); a ledger with entries and no register, or a missing register file, is a problem.
